Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the segmentation structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 570. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 132 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_570(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 570. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^132(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a segmentation algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 693. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 232/2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 693 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the segmentation structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 232. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
